300. ✅ **2026-06-04 - DS WAVE-CLEAR / AoE-shove scorer - the ELEVENTH scored axis, built by a 10-channel roster fan-out (ENGINE 1.111.0 -> 1.112.0; DS :8893 restarted taskkill pid 18736 + Start-ScheduledTask RC-DaemonSlayer -> 1.112.0 live-verified via /health + GET/POST /waveclear; RC NOT restarted - additive DS engine + new route + tests + Share + docs only).** Operator answered "go" to the recurring "next DS schema lift, fan out, exhaust, /done" cadence (12th use of the directive, 5th as an explicit multi-agent fan-out after items 294/297/298/299). The scaling/power-curve axis (item 299) was the last scored axis and EXHAUSTED, so an upfront AskUserQuestion forked the new axis (recommended-first) -> operator chose **wave-clear/AoE-shove** over effective-threat-range. **The ELEVENTH SCORED axis and the FIRST to score throughput against a wave of MINIONS** - every prior scored axis (DPS / burst / EHP / ability-DPS / healing-throughput / CC-output 294 / mobility 297 / sustain 298 / scaling 299 / the 6 archetype scorers) measures combat against a CHAMPION target; this owns the lane-priority / roam-window / objective-setup TEMPO dimension none of them measured. How fast and how safely a champion clears a wave and shoves: decisive ranged shovers (Zyra / Ziggs / AurelionSol / Heimerdinger / Brand / Sivir / Karthus) vs no-AoE last-hitters who concede priority (Vayne / Warwick / Yuumi / Zed / Talon). NEW `agents/daemon_slayer/waveclear.py`: a per-(champion, source) `WaveclearEntry` registry (source = P/Q/W/E/R or BASE) + `_WAVECLEAR_KIND_WEIGHT` 5-tier [FULL_AOE 1.0 / AOE_DOT 0.85 / MULTI_HIT 0.6 / CLEAVE_AA 0.5 / SINGLE_TARGET 0.25] + `_WAVECLEAR_RANGE_MULT` 3-tier shove-safety [GLOBAL 1.1 / RANGED 1.0 / MELEE 0.7 - a wave cleared from a safe distance shoves more tempo than one needing a walk into the minions] + `_WAVECLEAR_CONDITIONAL_PROB`=0.5 + `compute_waveclear` -> `WaveclearResult`. Each mechanism carries a 0..1 `magnitude` (share of the wave it removes); `waveclear_score` = sum of `kind_weight * range_mult * magnitude` (conditional credited at the 0.5 midpoint), `top_kind` labels the strongest single mechanism, `ranged_shove` is True when any RANGED/GLOBAL mechanism contributes (the safe-shove flag a tempo consumer keys on) + `WaveclearSourceEntry` per-source breakdown + to_dict. NEW `/waveclear` route (POST+GET) in `server.py` (import + `_route_waveclear` + `_POST_ROUTES` + HTML table; GET auto via `_GET_DISPATCH_ROUTES`). **PURELY ADDITIVE** - reads no existing scorer and is read by none -> every existing route byte-identical (the new endpoint IS the opt-in). EXHAUSTIVE 171-champ Workflow fan-out (10 classify + 10 completeness critics, pipeline, Sonnet, schema-validated, 705k subagent tokens / 33 tool uses / 167s) -> 376 entries / **171 champs (FULL roster coverage)** / 0 missing / 0 dropped / 27 conditional / kinds MULTI_HIT 173 / SINGLE_TARGET 94 / FULL_AOE 62 / AOE_DOT 31 / CLEAVE_AA 16 / bands RANGED 196 / MELEE 176 / GLOBAL 4 (Ezreal/Gangplank/Karthus/Ziggs); I wrote the engine + tests from the agents' structured output (PROBLEMS=0 - no hand-curation needed). + `tools/ds_waveclear_build.py` (reusable validate/inject generator: source in P/Q/W/E/R/BASE, kind + range_band known, magnitude in (0,1], emits keyword-arg `add()` grouped by champ in source order, splices the `# <ENTRIES>` marker LF-safe, writes the provenance sidecar) + `waveclear_registry_notes.json` (Share-excluded like `scaling_registry_notes.json` - added to the `ds_share_sync.py` exclusion tuple; it leaked into Share on the first sync, caught via git status, exclusion added + Share re-synced). Live `/waveclear` (GET+POST both verified): Ziggs 1.825 (Q FULL_AOE GLOBAL 0.9 = 0.99 + W MULTI_HIT RANGED 0.4 = 0.24 + E AOE_DOT RANGED 0.7 = 0.595) / Zyra 2.1175 (tops the roster) / Vayne 0.03 (no AoE). ENGINE 1.111.0 -> 1.112.0; **57 files / 65 version-pin syncs** (byte-level replace of `ENGINE_VERSION, "1.111.0"` -> 1.112.0 in 56 test files + `ENGINE_VERSION = "1.111.0"` in `__init__.py`; auto-skips Share/ + `_archive` + the historical docstring/route-doc provenance + the scaling notes JSON); +28 tests `test_waveclear_item300.py` (synthetic kind*range*magnitude*conditional math, hand-verified Zilean single-entry 0.85 + Ziggs 1.825 GLOBAL-band + Vayne low, dedicated-shover-vs-last-hitter directional, top_kind + ranged_shove + score=sum-of-sources identities, weight + range tiers, registry invariants 376/171 + GLOBAL=4, additive-route, ENGINE pin). The sustain/vamp-throughput axis (item 298) was the last scored axis and EXHAUSTED, so an upfront AskUserQuestion forked the new axis -> operator chose **scaling/power-curve** over wave-clear/AoE-shove or effective-threat-range. **The TENTH SCORED axis** alongside DPS / burst / EHP / ability-DPS / healing-throughput / CC-output (294) / mobility (297) / sustain (298) / the 6 archetype scorers - and the ONLY one that owns the TIME dimension (every other scored axis is a static now-snapshot of one capability): a champion's power TRAJECTORY across the game, weak-now-hypercarry-late (Veigar / Kassadin / Vayne / Nasus / Kayle - high late power + steep positive slope) vs early-lane-bully-falls-off (Renekton / Draven / Pantheon / LeeSin / Elise - high early + NEGATIVE slope). NEW `agents/daemon_slayer/scaling.py`: a per-(champion, source) `ScalingEntry` registry (source = P/Q/W/E/R or BASE) + `_SCALING_KIND_WEIGHT` 5-tier [INFINITE_STACK 1.0 / FORM_SPIKE 0.8 / RATIO_HYPERSCALE 0.8 / ITEM_RELIANT 0.5 / EARLY_FRONTLOAD 0.4] + per-kind early/mid/late ramp `_SCALING_STAGE_RAMP` (a stack/ratio/item kind RAMPS UP to 1.0 late; FORM_SPIKE flat once unlocked; EARLY_FRONTLOAD (1.0,0.55,0.25) DECAYS) + `compute_scaling` -> `ScalingResult`. Each mechanism tags the `online_stage` it keys on (EARLY/MID/LATE - `_stage_contribution` returns 0 for any stage earlier than that) + a 0..1 `magnitude`; folded into early_power/mid_power/late_power, conditional mechanisms discounted by `_SCALING_CONDITIONAL_PROB`=0.5. `scaling_score` = late_power (conventional higher-is-stronger headline); `scaling_slope` = late_power - early_power (the SIGNED trajectory: + scales up, - falls off, ~0 even). NEW `agents/daemon_slayer/mobility.py`: `MobilityEntry` registry + `_MOBILITY_KIND_WEIGHT` 5-tier [BLINK 1.0 / DASH 0.8 / LEAP 0.8 / UNTARGET_REPOSITION 0.7 / MS_STEROID 0.5] + `compute_mobility` -> `MobilityResult` (`mobility_score` unconditional weighted units + `conditional_mobility_score` gated at `_MOBILITY_CONDITIONAL_PROB`=0.5 + `total_mobility_score` + `raw_mobility_units`). Normalised to Flash-units via `_FLASH_UNITS`=400 (1.0 unit = a Flash): a displacement = `min(range, _MAX_DASH_UNITS=1500)/400 * charges`; an MS_STEROID = `ms_pct * _BASE_MS(330) * dur / 400`, with `dur` defaulting to `_MS_SUSTAINED_WINDOW_S`=3.0 for a sustained/toggle buff (Quinn R / MissFortune Strut / Warwick W) carrying no fixed window. A SELF window during which the champion cannot be damaged or killed voids ALL incoming damage for `window_s`, so it adds the avoided `window_s / _FIGHT_WINDOW_S` (6.0s) damage FRACTION to the EHP numerator - FINITE (never literally infinite), capped at the whole fight, amortized by availability. The revived FRACTION is EXACT from ability text (Anivia "restores ALL health" -> 1.0; Zac "revived with 10:50% max health" by level -> level_scaled); only the availability+survival midpoint (`_REVIVE_PROB=0.4` - long CD + must-survive-the-resurrection-window) is the assumption (Phase D feeds live passive-CD + egg survival). **EXHAUSTIVE roster scan** (all 171 champs, death-triggered self-revive restoring a sustained HP pool): exactly **Anivia P Rebirth** (full HP, 240s CD -> x1.40) + **Zac P Cell Division** (10:50% by level, 300s CD, bloblet-gated -> x1.04 L1 to x1.20 L18). DOCUMENTED EXCLUSIONS by class: post-death decaying frenzy restoring NO sustained pool (Sion P "100% then -2.3:24.4/0.264s, healing 0% effective, always dies", Karthus P / KogMaw P zombie cast/bomb window), ally-targeted revive (Zilean R / Renata W / Akshan W - rides an ally, the Orianna-E class), GA item revive (item-side, out of (champion,key,form) scope), 6 false positives (Annie/Aurora/Draven/Yone/Shaco/Ekko). Build-independent numerator multiplier (uniform scale) so flag-on does NOT re-rank an item-ranker - makes per-row EHP scalars accurate (mirrors the DR registry). Threaded through both EHP-bearing scorers (`rank_items_by_ehp` + `rank_items_by_hybrid`) + `compute_hybrid` + the `/ehp` `/rank-tank` `/hybrid` `/rank-bruiser` routes. Live `/ehp` proof: Anivia L11 blended 2011.42 off -> 2815.98 on (mult 1.40); Caitlyn off==on byte-identical. +25 tests `test_passive_revive_overrides_item288.py`; DS suite 6179 -> 6204 passed / 1 skip / 1 xfail / 1936 subtests; ruff clean; ASCII-only; 56 ENGINE pin syncs; Share re-synced 282 files --check clean + Share/CHANGELOG + Share/docs/04 (revive moved Included) + 05 counts. **Don't-redo:** a REVIVE is the canonical home for a death-triggered second HP pool (NUMERATOR multiplier, NOT a resist/DR denominator term - do NOT model Anivia's egg armor/MR as a resist grant, that stays the item-272 exclusion); the revive registry is EXHAUSTED (Anivia + Zac the SOLE sustained-pool self-revives); Sion/Karthus/KogMaw are decaying-frenzy NON-revives (do NOT seed); ally revives (Zilean/Renata/Akshan) + Orianna E need an ally-buff seam the self-EHP scorer does not score; `_REVIVE_PROB` 0.4 + Zac bloblet-survival are operator-tunable Phase-D midpoints. **NEXT (Phase D, live-gated):** flip `apply_passive_revive` default-on per champ after a "saner not different" re-rank; feed the live passive-cooldown + a real egg/bloblet-survival estimate. 218. ✅ **2026-05-30 - item 218 SHIPPED: competitor lift #5 matchup cooldown-watch card (1 commit; non-engine; non-frozen; no ENGINE bump; no DS restart; RC restarted pid 14944 -> 20228 for new route module).** Operator picked lift #1 (cooldown-watch) from the item-217 competitor-lift gate slate via AskUserQuestion scope fork. Pure presentation JOIN over data RC already owns - NO new compute, NO ENGINE math change, NO schema lift, NO new dep. **Shipped:** NEW engine module `agents/daemon_slayer/cooldown_watch.py` `compute_cooldown_watch(roster, top_n=5) -> CooldownWatchResult`: per ENEMY champion, joins the single highest-threat hard-CC ability (longest max-rank duration across `_PER_SPELL_CC_DURATIONS` unconditional + `cc_conditional.get_conditional_entries` probability-gated) to its max-rank base `cooldown` from `data/daemon_slayer/16.11.1/champion_abilities.json` (patch-pinned loader mirrors `cc_pressure._load_tenacity_map`; module-level `_ABILITIES` monkeypatchable for tests). Headline cd = `cooldown[-1]` (max-rank) paired with `durations[-1]` (max-rank CC); full per-rank cd tuple exposed. Mode-agnostic (cooldown intrinsic to the ability; ARAM tenacity scales INCOMING CC on the modified champ, not outgoing ability fact) so the module/route take NO mode param. Sort: cc_duration DESC, tiebreak cooldown ASC (lower cd = more frequent threat), then champ. 211. ✅ **2026-05-28 (Home Recent-5 wrong-items bug) - item 211 SHIPPED: gameId-keyed ingest + backfill + Match-V5 recovery (3 commits `b7bd74a` + `4e043a8` + `1628a47` pushed origin/main `4ff288f..1628a47`; non-frozen; RC restarted pid 16768 -> 11272 alive=True reload_ok=True).** Operator: "recent 5 is always missing an item row for one of the cards - and eventually items are on the wrong matches". Root cause = `dashboard/routes_last_match.py:122-125` `_serve_last_match_ingest` SELECTed `ORDER BY timestamp DESC LIMIT 1`. Race: Game-PC LCU agent POSTs ingest on EndOfGame BEFORE local `performance_tracker.save_match` writes the new row -> ingest stamped the PREVIOUS row's raw_data with the new game's lcu_match_detail+items. New row then never received ingest -> items=[]. Visible live: row 1690 (Jinx 22:54:03) carried Caitlyn's gid 5569828374; row 1691 (Caitlyn 23:57:12) had items=[]. **`b7bd74a` fix(ingest):** matches schema gains `game_id INTEGER DEFAULT 0` + `idx_matches_game_id` + in-place ALTER TABLE migration. Fresh-vs-legacy CREATE/ALTER branch in `core/match_db.py:84-110` required because full _SCHEMA's CREATE INDEX statements explode against narrow legacy tables. Route rewritten: gameId-primary -> gameCreation+gameDuration vs row.timestamp +/- 180s for legacy rows -> 202+queue+60s retry/10s drain via lazy background thread. Matched rows stamp game_id so re-ingest is idempotent. `performance_tracker.save_match` persists game_id when Live Client exposes it (most modes report 0; ts-window fallback covers). +11 TDD tests in `tests/test_last_match_ingest_gameid.py` (schema migration fresh + legacy ALTER / gameId match + idempotence + auto-stamp / ts-window fallback + outside-window=202 / queue drain + age-out / item 211 race regression pin / ASCII hygiene). RC suite 3758 pass + 2 pre-existing failed (Pantheon SR lifeline clash item 168 + Jinx sr-bruiser path item 178; NOT regressions). **`4e043a8` chore(backfill):** `tools/backfill_match_ingest_misattribution.py` walks every non-TFT row carrying lcu_match_detail, extracts operator championId via tracked_puuid -> participantIdentities -> participants[me_pid].championId, maps via DDragon to display name, compares vs db.champion. Three verdicts: MATCH (stamp game_id), MISMATCH (find target row by champ + ts window, MOVE detail with forensic stamps `item211_backfill_moved_to/received_from`, clear source), ORPHAN (no target within window - log + leave). Atomic file-copy backup before any UPDATE. `--dry-run` + `--window <s>` flags. Applied 180s pass: 17 correct + 9 moved + 21 orphans. Re-applied 600s pass for slow-grader cases: 26 correct + 15 moved + 15 true orphans. Backups at `data/match_history.db.bak-item211-20260528-{201639,201739}`. **`1628a47` feat(recovery):** `tools/recover_match_via_match_v5.py` for orphans where ingest NEVER landed (Game-PC LCU agent offline at EndOfGame / RC down / POST failed). Account-V1 lookup re-resolves current puuid (matches.tracked_puuid is STALE for older rows per [[reference_riot_puuid_rotation]] - operator's puuid rotated post-2026-05-20 SamplePlayer#Vayne -> SamplePlayer#Trist). Match-V5 by-puuid in +/- 30 min of row's db.timestamp; filter by championName == db.champion; fetch full detail; synthesize LCU-shaped blob with participantIdentities + participants[].stats.itemN; POST /api/last-match/ingest. Synth blob carries `_source = "match_v5_recovery_item211"` + `_puuid_used` for forensics. `_normalize_unit(n)` handles Match-V5 gameDuration unit drift (seconds when gameEndTimestamp present, milliseconds in older payloads; divides by 1000 when n > 100_000). Row 1690 Jinx recovered via NA1_5569790114 -> items=[1055, 3032, 2523, 3031, 3086, 3144]. **Verified live:** Home Recent-5 5/5 cards correct after all 3 commits (Caitlyn 00:30:37 / Caitlyn 23:57:12 / Jinx 22:54:03 / MF 22:14:56 / Samira 21:46:50 all show their own champion's items). RC restarted via restart_trigger.txt for route module reload. `py -m ruff check .` ALL CHECKS PASSED. `py -m py_compile` clean on all touched files. **Don't-redo:** (a) `_serve_last_match_ingest` row-match contract is gameId-primary then ts-window then queue+retry. The fresh-vs-legacy CREATE/ALTER branch in `core/match_db.py:84-110` is REQUIRED because full _SCHEMA's CREATE INDEX statements explode against narrow legacy tables (test_alter_legacy_db_adds_game_id_column pins). (e) Backfill `--window 180` (default) covers normal local-writer cadence; `--window 600` covers slow-grader cases (operator's setup runs grader for up to ~3min post-game). 209. ✅ **2026-05-27 (post-game ranked SR queue) - item 209 SHIPPED: bridge watcher + LCU phase watcher CREATE_NO_WINDOW / capture-reliability fix (2 commits + 2 merges + Game-PC redeploy + 2 watcher restarts; `8d36960` + `6f56cd6` pushed origin/main `07f5e79..6f56cd6`; 1 operator-granted frozen file edit; no RC restart - non-frozen + non-coach-prompt edits).** Two distinct bugs surfaced + fixed in one session. **209a `8d36960` fix(bridge): CREATE_NO_WINDOW on watcher claude.cmd spawns (3 files / +95 / -2).** Operator-reported "a cmd window keeps opening on legion". Root cause = `tools/bridge_watcher.py:269` `_send_push_notification` + `tools/bridge_watcher_actions.py:244` `_run_subprocess` both spawned `claude.cmd` (npm shim) without `creationflags=subprocess.CREATE_NO_WINDOW`. pythonw parent has no console so spawning a .cmd allocated a new visible cmd.exe window for the duration of each ~claude --print call. Anomaly state showed 10 escalations / 24h + push_notif throttled to 3/hr = visible cmd window flashes every escalation. **Fix:** 1-line `_flags = subprocess.CREATE_NO_WINDOW if sys.platform == "win32" else 0` + pass via `creationflags=_flags` on both spawns. `tools/bridge_watcher_actions.py` is FROZEN per CLAUDE.md L53 - operator-granted edit. Mirrors the flag pattern at `tools/legion_bridge_daemon.py:212`. NEW `tests/test_bridge_watcher_create_no_window.py` 3 tests (AST-walk drift guards on both spawn sites + ASCII hygiene). RC-BridgeWatcher restarted pid 12000 -> 12228 alive heartbeat fresh. **209b `6f56cd6` fix(gamepc): phase watcher capture reliability - drop WaitingForStats + InProgress edge delay (2 files / +53 / -10).** OPERATOR DIAGNOSIS: the item 207 LCU phase watcher fires DXGI capture (both monitors via bettercam) on the `WaitingForStats` gameflow phase which lands at the exact moment League swaps 1920x1080 -> 1440p. A bound DXGI grab at that resolution-swap moment is unreliable - same class as the retired `gamepc_screen_agent.py` continuous DXGI loop. The `Matchmaking -> InProgress` transition edge is the symmetric game-start swap risk. **Surgical fix:** `tools/gamepc_phase_watcher.py:109` `_CAPTURED_GAMEFLOW_PHASES` DROP `"WaitingForStats"` (keep ChampSelect + InProgress only); NEW `INPROGRESS_CAPTURE_DELAY_S = 15.0` module constant at L118; `handle_event` sleeps past the game-start resolution swap before binding DXGI when classified topic is gameflow_phase/InProgress. Cherry augment_select stays (mid-game stable resolution; capture-safe). Comment cites the resolution-swap rationale + DO-NOT-RE-ADD note. 65/65 tests PASS (+2/-1): NEW `test_waiting_for_stats_NOT_captured_item_209` drift guard + `test_inprogress_capture_delay_constant_defined`. Game-PC binary patched via HTTP-pull dance per [[reference_gamepc_http_server_redeploy]]: sha256 `66298e8c475b59353ca697178b6c7e5e6e6c6c98b5964cf01f1e068e05204e71` verified; `RC-PhaseWatcher` restarted pid 5128 -> 14320 alive=Running. Captured the resolution-swap capture-reliability rule + MEMORY.md index updated. **Don't-redo:** (a) Both bridge_watcher spawn sites are now CI-locked by drift guard - do NOT remove creationflags. (b) `_CAPTURED_GAMEFLOW_PHASES` MUST NOT re-add `WaitingForStats` (drift guard fails). PGR captures must come from a trigger that fires AFTER the resolution swap settles (e.g. dashboard `/api/state` debounce), not from the WAMP edge. (c) `INPROGRESS_CAPTURE_DELAY_S = 15.0` is calibrated for the typical Windows 1440p<->1080p swap; tests monkey-patch to 0. Future event-capture work on Game-PC MUST exclude any phase that coincides with a resolution swap - the discrete-event mental model ("only fires once at game end") is wrong; the trigger is the resolution swap co-firing with a bound DXGI surface. (d) Cherry augment_select capture stays safe (mid-game stable resolution); do NOT re-pitch removing it. (e) The item 207 phase watcher's own header comment said "NEVER reintroduce the continuous screen-agent loop" - but a DXGI grab at the game-end resolution-swap moment is the same unreliable surface compressed to one frame, not a different surface. Both are now excluded. **Carries forward:** (a) `tools/bridge_watcher_install.ps1` deployment includes both fixes by default going forward. (b) The Game-PC `gamepc_phase_watcher.py` deployed binary is patched; future redeploys via HTTP-pull dance inherit the fix. (c) Frozen-file grant USED for `tools/bridge_watcher_actions.py` only; NOT used for other frozen files this session. (d) Item 208 carries unchanged.
